This roundup covers 509 Microsoft security vulnerabilities. CVSS base scores range from 4.3 to 10.0. None are reported as actively exploited at the time of release. The issues affect ASP.NET Core, Access, Active Directory Federation Services, Azure API Center, Azure App Service, Azure CycleCloud, Azure Event Grid, Azure SRE Agent, BitLocker, Bluetooth Driver, Bookings, CCFFilter Driver, COM, Connected Devices Platform Service, DNS Server, Dataverse, Defender for Endpoint, Desktop Window Manager, Domain Name Service, Dynamics 365 Business Central, Dynamics ## CVE-2026-96940

Microsoft Exchange Server contains a vulnerability due to weak authorization that allows an authenticated attacker to elevate their privileges over the network.

Affected products:
- Exchange Server

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-96940

## CVE-2026-69435

CVE-2026-69435 is a vulnerability in the Microsoft Azure SRE Agent caused by missing authorization checks. This flaw allows an authenticated attacker to perform unauthorized actions and escalate privileges over the network.

Affected products:
- Azure SRE Agent

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-69435

## CVE-2026-77900

A vulnerability in Microsoft Azure App Service allows an unauthorized remote attacker to execute arbitrary code due to missing authentication for a critical function. This flaw carries a high CVSS base score of 9.8 and facilitates RCE over a network without requiring prior authentication.

Affected products:
- Azure App Service

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-77900

## CVE-2026-88131

CVE-2026-88131 is a critical vulnerability in Microsoft Dataverse involving insecure deserialization of untrusted data, which allows an unauthenticated remote attacker to achieve remote code execution (RCE) over a network.

Affected products:
- Dataverse

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-88131

## CVE-2026-94510

Microsoft Bookings contains an authorization bypass vulnerability (CVE-2026-94510) caused by a user-controlled key, which allows an unauthenticated attacker to perform privilege escalation over a network. The vulnerability carries a high CVSS base score of 9.9, indicating critical severity.

Affected products:
- Bookings

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-94510

## CVE-2026-96207

CVE-2026-96207 describes a vulnerability in Microsoft Partner Center involving improper certificate validation. This flaw allows an unauthorized, network-adjacent attacker to perform privilege escalation within the platform.

Affected products:
- Partner Center

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-96207

## CVE-2026-83943

CVE-2026-83943 is an information exposure vulnerability in Microsoft Azure API Center that allows an unauthorized attacker to access sensitive information over a network. The vulnerability has a CVSS v3.1 base score of 8.7, indicating a high risk of unauthorized data disclosure.

Affected products:
- Azure API Center

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-83943

## CVE-2026-83947

CVE-2026-83947 describes a missing authorization vulnerability in Azure Event Grid. This flaw allows an authenticated attacker to perform spoofing attacks over a network, potentially leading to unauthorized data handling or service manipulation within the event-driven architecture.

Affected products:
- Azure Event Grid

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-83947

## CVE-2026-108744

The pbi-cli tool is vulnerable to OS command injection due to the improper handling of .pbip file paths within the desktop_sync.py module. When reopening projects, the application passes unquoted file paths to 'cmd /c start', allowing an attacker to execute arbitrary commands by crafting a malicious path containing shell metacharacters such as '&'. This vulnerability results in command execution with the privileges of the user running the application.

Affected products:
- pbi-cli (3.10.1 through 3.12.0)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-108744

## CVE-2026-100202

## CVE-2026-97816

CVE-2026-97816 is a critical use-after-free vulnerability in OpenSSH for Windows that enables an unauthorized remote attacker to achieve remote code execution. The vulnerability is exploitable over the network without authentication, warranting immediate attention for patching and monitoring for exploitation attempts against SSH services.

Affected products:
- OpenSSH for Windows

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-97816

## CVE-2026-100200

CVE-2026-100200 is a use-after-free vulnerability in the Windows Update Service that enables a locally authenticated attacker to achieve privilege escalation. The vulnerability exists within the service's memory management logic, allowing an attacker to execute arbitrary code or gain higher-level permissions on the local system.

Affected products:
- Windows Update Service

- Windows Client License Service

- Windows Update Service

- Sysinternals

## CVE-2026-100207

CVE-2026-100207 describes an OS command injection vulnerability in Microsoft Azure CycleCloud. The vulnerability allows an authorized attacker to execute arbitrary code locally due to improper neutralization of special elements used in OS commands, potentially leading to unauthorized system control.

Affected products:
- Azure CycleCloud

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-100207

## CVE-2026-100220

CVE-2026-100220 is a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability residing in the Windows Media Video Decoder. This flaw allows a remote, unauthorized attacker to trigger arbitrary code execution by sending a specially crafted video stream to the affected component over a network.

Affected products:
- Windows Media Video Decoder

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-100220

- Defender for Endpoint

- Resilient File System (ReFS)

CVE-2026-105717 is a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability in Microsoft Office Access. An unauthenticated attacker can exploit this flaw to achieve remote code execution over a network. - Office Access

## CVE-2026-105718

CVE-2026-105718 describes an out-of-bounds read vulnerability in Microsoft Excel that can be leveraged by a remote unauthorized attacker to achieve remote code execution over a network. Detection should focus on monitoring anomalous network traffic originating from the Excel process and identifying unexpected child process creation or unauthorized file access patterns related to office applications.

Affected products:
- Excel

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-105718

CVE-2026-105728 describes an improper authentication vulnerability in Microsoft Dynamics Business Central. Affected products:
- Dynamics Business Central

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-105728

## CVE-2026-108133

A vulnerability in Windows Modern Device Management (MDM) involves the release of an invalid pointer or reference, which can be exploited by an authorized local attacker to achieve privilege escalation.

Affected products:
- Windows Modern Device Management

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-108133

## CVE-2026-108134

A use-after-free vulnerability exists in the Windows Autopilot component of the Windows operating system. An authorized local attacker can exploit this flaw to execute code or perform actions with elevated privileges on the target system.

Affected products:
- Windows Autopilot

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-108134

## CVE-2026-108135

CVE-2026-108135 is a local privilege escalation vulnerability in the Windows Backup Engine caused by improper link resolution before file access. An authorized local attacker can exploit this flaw to gain higher privileges by leveraging the 'link following' vulnerability during the backup process.

Affected products:
- Windows Backup Engine

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-108135

## CVE-2026-108136

CVE-2026-108136 is a use-after-free vulnerability within the Microsoft Windows Search Component that can be exploited by an authenticated local attacker to achieve privilege escalation on the affected system.

Affected products:
- Windows Search Component

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-108136

Affected products:
- Windows Modern Device Management

## CVE-2026-108400

CVE-2026-108400 describes an integer overflow or wraparound vulnerability in the Microsoft Windows Codecs Library. This vulnerability allows a remote, unauthorized attacker to execute arbitrary code over a network, potentially leading to full system compromise.

Affected products:
- Windows Codecs Library

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-108400

## CVE-2026-108457

CVE-2026-108457 is an out-of-bounds write vulnerability in the Microsoft Windows PDF component that allows an unauthorized, network-based attacker to achieve remote code execution on the target system.

Affected products:
- Windows PDF

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-108457

## CVE-2026-108458

CVE-2026-108458 is a use-after-free vulnerability in the Microsoft Remote Desktop Client that allows an unauthorized attacker to achieve remote code execution over a network. This flaw represents a critical security risk as it can be triggered by an attacker without prior authentication.

Affected products:
- Remote Desktop Client

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-108458

CVE-2026-108460 is a use-after-free vulnerability in the Windows Message Queuing (MSMQ) component. An unauthorized remote attacker can leverage this flaw to achieve arbitrary code execution over a network, presenting a significant risk to affected Windows systems.

Affected products:
- Windows Message Queuing

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-108460

## CVE-2026-108463

CVE-2026-108463 is a vulnerability in Windows SmartScreen involving improper resolution of path equivalence, which allows an unauthorized remote attacker to bypass security checks over a network. Detection engineers should monitor for abnormal path resolutions or bypassed security warnings associated with file execution originating from network locations.

Affected products:
- Windows SmartScreen

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-108463

## CVE-2026-108464

A use-after-free vulnerability in the Microsoft Software Protection Platform (SPP) allows a locally authenticated attacker to escalate privileges. The vulnerability occurs due to improper memory management, enabling the execution of arbitrary code or elevated system actions.

Affected products:
- Software Protection Platform

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-108464

## CVE-2026-108465

CVE-2026-108465 is a use-after-free vulnerability in the Windows Update Delivery Optimization component that allows an authorized local attacker to achieve privilege escalation.

Affected products:
- Windows Update Delivery Optimization

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-108465

## CVE-2026-49785

CVE-2026-49785 is a security feature bypass vulnerability in Windows UEFI caused by improper resource shutdown or release. An authorized local attacker can exploit this flaw to bypass Secure Boot protections, potentially allowing the execution of unauthorized or malicious code during the boot process.

Affected products:
- Windows UEFI

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-49785

## CVE-2026-72968

CVE-2026-72968 is a privilege escalation vulnerability in the Windows Program Compatibility Assistant Service caused by a use-after-free flaw. An authenticated attacker can exploit this vulnerability locally to execute code with elevated permissions.

Affected products:
- Windows Program Compatibility Assistant Service

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-72968

## CVE-2026-69935

CVE-2026-69935 is a use-after-free vulnerability within the Windows Storage component that allows an authorized local attacker to escalate their privileges. Detection efforts should focus on anomalous processes interacting with low-level storage APIs or unexpected privilege transitions initiated by local users.

Affected products:
- Windows Storage

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69935
















































































































































































































































































































































































Related in this roundup: [CVE-2026-69766](#cve-2026-69766), [CVE-2026-83911](#cve-2026-83911).

## CVE-2026-69795

CVE-2026-69795 is a privilege escalation vulnerability in the Windows Clipboard Server caused by a null pointer dereference. An authorized local attacker can exploit this flaw to gain elevated privileges on the affected Windows system.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69795

- Windows

## CVE-2026-69968

CVE-2026-69968 is a relative path traversal vulnerability affecting the libarchive library on Windows systems. The flaw allows an unauthenticated, remote attacker to perform file tampering by manipulating file paths during archive extraction or processing.

Affected products:
- libarchive

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69968

## CVE-2026-69970

CVE-2026-69970 is a local elevation of privilege vulnerability in the Microsoft Streaming Service caused by a heap-based buffer overflow. An authorized attacker can exploit this flaw to execute code with higher privileges on the target system.

Affected products:
- Streaming Service

## CVE-2026-69969

CVE-2026-69969 is an information disclosure vulnerability in the Windows AppX Deployment Service caused by improper link resolution ('link following') prior to file access. An authorized attacker could exploit this flaw to gain unauthorized access to sensitive information on the local system.

- Windows AppX Deployment Service

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69969

- Windows Kernel-Mode Drivers

- Windows

## CVE-2026-69978

CVE-2026-69978 is a denial-of-service vulnerability in the Windows VMSwitch component triggered by a null pointer dereference. An attacker with local access can exploit this flaw to cause the system to stop responding.

## CVE-2026-70010

- Windows

## CVE-2026-70014

A use-after-free vulnerability in Microsoft Windows Cryptographic Services allows an authorized attacker to achieve remote code execution over a network. This flaw represents a critical security risk to the integrity and availability of Windows systems, requiring immediate patching as identified by Microsoft Security Response Center.

Affected products:
- Windows Cryptographic Services

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70014

## CVE-2026-70015

A use-after-free vulnerability in the Windows Local Security Authority Subsystem Service (LSASS) allows a locally authenticated attacker to elevate their privileges to a higher level of authority on the system.

Affected products:
- Windows Local Security Authority Subsystem Service (LSASS)

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70015

Affected products:
- Windows Secure Kernel Mode

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70017

## CVE-2026-70026

- Windows

## CVE-2026-70033

CVE-2026-70033 is a denial of service vulnerability in the Windows Remote Desktop Gateway (RD Gateway) service. The issue stems from a failure to properly release memory after its effective lifetime, which can be triggered by an unauthorized attacker over a network to cause the service to crash or become unavailable.

Affected products:
- Windows Remote Desktop Gateway

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70033

## CVE-2026-70056

A use-after-free vulnerability in the Windows Update Service allows a local, authorized attacker to escalate privileges. The vulnerability resides within the Windows Update service component, enabling an attacker to gain higher levels of access on an already compromised system.

Affected products:
- Windows Update Service

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70056

## CVE-2026-70057

A security feature bypass vulnerability exists in the Windows Routing and Remote Access Service (RRAS) due to incorrect authorization, allowing an authenticated local attacker to bypass security restrictions.

Affected products:
- Routing and Remote Access Service

## CVE-2026-70058

CVE-2026-70058 is an out-of-bounds write vulnerability within the Windows DHCP Server component. An unauthenticated remote attacker can exploit this flaw to achieve remote code execution by sending specifically crafted network packets to the targeted DHCP service.

Affected products:
- Windows DHCP Server

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70058

A double-free vulnerability exists within the Windows implementation of libarchive, which can be leveraged by a remote unauthorized attacker to achieve remote code execution via specially crafted input processed over a network.

- libarchive

## CVE-2026-70076

CVE-2026-70076 describes a use-after-free vulnerability within the Windows Win32k kernel subsystem. This flaw allows an attacker who has already gained initial authorization on a system to execute arbitrary code or perform operations with elevated privileges, effectively escalating their access rights on the local machine.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70076

- DNS Server

- Windows

## CVE-2026-70117

A heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability in the Windows Win32k kernel subsystem allows a local, authorized attacker to elevate their privileges to higher system levels. Exploitation of this vulnerability involves memory corruption in the kernel, potentially granting unauthorized access or control over the affected system.

Affected products:
- Windows

## CVE-2026-70114

CVE-2026-70114 is a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability in the Windows Kernel that enables a locally authenticated attacker to escalate privileges. Detection engineering should focus on monitoring for abnormal kernel-mode memory access patterns or unexpected elevation of process tokens.

Affected products:
- Windows Kernel

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70114

## CVE-2026-70127

CVE-2026-70127 is a use-after-free vulnerability in the Microsoft Windows Domain Name Service (DNS) that allows an unauthenticated, remote attacker to execute arbitrary code. The vulnerability exists due to improper memory management, and exploitation can be achieved by sending a specially crafted request to a vulnerable DNS server.

Affected products:
- Windows Domain Name Service

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70127

Affected products:
- Graphics Component

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70123

- Windows Portable Device File System Driver

- Windows Audio Service

## CVE-2026-70126

CVE-2026-70126 describes an out-of-bounds read vulnerability in the Windows Bluetooth Port Driver that can be leveraged by an unauthorized attacker over an adjacent network to achieve remote code execution. Detection engineers should monitor for anomalous Bluetooth traffic patterns or unexpected memory access errors originating from the Bluetooth driver stack.

Affected products:
- Windows Bluetooth Port Driver

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70126

- Windows Remote POS Driver

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70135

- Windows DNS

## CVE-2026-70205

A type confusion vulnerability exists in the Windows Ancillary Function Driver for WinSock (AFD.sys). An authenticated local attacker can exploit this vulnerability to achieve elevation of privilege by manipulating resource types during processing.

## CVE-2026-70255

CVE-2026-70255 is a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability in the Windows Desktop Window Manager that enables a locally authenticated attacker to escalate their privileges. Detection should focus on monitoring for unauthorized attempts to access or manipulate DWM processes or system-level handles by unprivileged users.

Affected products:
- Desktop Window Manager

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70255

## CVE-2026-70252

The Virtual Hard Disk (VHD) Miniport Driver in Microsoft Windows contains a use-after-free vulnerability that allows an authenticated local attacker to escalate privileges on the affected system.

Affected products:
- Windows

## CVE-2026-70272

CVE-2026-70272 is a local privilege escalation vulnerability in the Windows Installer component, stemming from a use-after-free flaw. An authorized local attacker can exploit this vulnerability to gain elevated privileges on the system.

Affected products:
- Windows Installer

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70272

## CVE-2026-69521

CVE-2026-69521 is a critical vulnerability in the Windows iSCSI component caused by an integer overflow or wraparound, which allows a remote, unauthenticated attacker to achieve remote code execution on affected Windows systems.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69521

## CVE-2026-69644

CVE-2026-69644 is an information disclosure vulnerability in the Windows iSCSI component. It allows a locally authenticated attacker to gain unauthorized access to sensitive system information, facilitating potential credential or data harvesting.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69644

- BitLocker

## CVE-2026-83499

CVE-2026-83499 is a privilege escalation vulnerability in Windows Server Update Service caused by insufficient granularity of access control. An authorized attacker can exploit this flaw locally to gain elevated privileges on the affected host.

- Windows Server Update Service

## CVE-2026-83516

CVE-2026-83516 is a denial of service vulnerability in the Windows Local Security Authority Subsystem Service (LSASS) caused by an out-of-bounds read. An unauthenticated remote attacker can trigger this vulnerability to crash the LSASS process, leading to a system-wide denial of service.

Affected products:
- Local Security Authority Subsystem Service

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83516

## CVE-2026-83706

CVE-2026-83706 is a local privilege escalation vulnerability in the Windows NTFS file system caused by a use-after-free defect. An authorized local attacker can exploit this flaw to execute code with higher privileges.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83706

## CVE-2026-83707

CVE-2026-83707 is a local privilege escalation vulnerability in Windows Event Tracing caused by a use-after-free flaw. An authorized attacker can exploit this condition to execute arbitrary code with elevated privileges on the target system.

- Windows

## CVE-2026-83708

CVE-2026-83708 is a stack-based buffer overflow vulnerability in the Windows Installer service that allows an authenticated local attacker to escalate privileges. Detection should focus on monitoring for unusual process creation or service behavior originating from the Windows Installer service process.

Affected products:
- Windows Installer

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83708

## CVE-2026-83712

- Windows

- Windows Kernel

- Windows

## CVE-2026-83843

CVE-2026-83843 is an out-of-bounds read vulnerability in the Windows Internet Key Exchange (IKE) extension, enabling an unauthenticated, remote attacker to trigger a denial of service condition on affected Windows systems by sending specially crafted network packets.

Affected products:
- Windows Internet Key Exchange (IKE) Extension

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83843

## CVE-2026-83844

CVE-2026-83844 is an out-of-bounds read vulnerability in the Windows Paint application that can be exploited by a remote attacker to achieve code execution. Detection efforts should focus on anomalous network traffic or unusual process spawning behavior initiated by the Paint application.

Affected products:
- Paint

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83844

## CVE-2026-83849

CVE-2026-83849 is an out-of-bounds read vulnerability in Windows Hello that allows an authorized local attacker to perform information disclosure. Detection engineers should monitor for unauthorized access attempts to biometric data processing or sensitive system memory areas associated with the Windows Hello authentication subsystem.

Affected products:
- Windows Hello

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83849

## CVE-2026-83853

CVE-2026-83853 is a use-after-free vulnerability within the Windows Hello component that allows an attacker with authorized local access to escalate their privileges on the host system.

Affected products:
- Windows Hello

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83853

## CVE-2026-83854

CVE-2026-83854 describes an information disclosure vulnerability in the Windows Notification component caused by improper access control. An authorized local attacker can exploit this flaw to gain unauthorized access to sensitive information.

Affected products:
- Windows

## CVE-2026-83855

CVE-2026-83855 is a denial-of-service vulnerability in the Windows WLAN Service caused by an integer underflow flaw. This vulnerability allows an unauthorized remote attacker to disrupt WLAN functionality by triggering a wraparound condition in the service, leading to a crash or service unavailability.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83855

- Windows

## CVE-2026-83865

CVE-2026-83865 identifies a relative path traversal vulnerability in the Windows Internet (wininet.dll) component. This flaw allows an authorized local attacker to perform file tampering, potentially leading to unauthorized modification of system files or security bypasses.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83865

## CVE-2026-83875

CVE-2026-83875 is an out-of-bounds read vulnerability in the Windows HID Parser Library. An authorized local attacker can exploit this flaw to read sensitive information from the memory space, leading to unauthorized information disclosure.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83875

Affected products:
- Bluetooth Driver

## CVE-2026-83867

CVE-2026-83867 is a local privilege escalation vulnerability in the Windows Push Notifications service caused by a heap-based buffer overflow. An attacker with local authorization can exploit this flaw to execute code with higher privileges.

Affected products:
- Windows Push Notifications

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83867

## CVE-2026-83879

CVE-2026-83879 is a local privilege escalation vulnerability in the Windows Overlay Filter caused by a race condition during concurrent execution with improper resource synchronization. An authorized attacker can exploit this flaw to gain elevated privileges on the affected Windows system.

Affected products:
- Windows Overlay Filter

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83879

## CVE-2026-83885

CVE-2026-83885 is a security vulnerability in Windows Virtualization-Based Security (VBS) Enclave due to inadequate encryption strength, allowing a local authenticated attacker to conduct spoofing attacks.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83885

## CVE-2026-83892

CVE-2026-83892 describes an out-of-bounds read vulnerability in the Windows HID Parser Library. An authorized attacker with local access could exploit this flaw to disclose sensitive information from the system memory.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83892

## CVE-2026-83893

CVE-2026-83893 is an information disclosure vulnerability in the Windows Clipboard User Service. An authorized attacker with local access can exploit this flaw to read sensitive system information, potentially leading to the exposure of data to unauthorized control spheres.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83893

- Windows

## CVE-2026-70163

CVE-2026-70163 describes a denial-of-service vulnerability in Microsoft's implementation of OpenSSH for Windows. The flaw stems from improper resource allocation management, specifically a lack of limits or throttling, which allows an unauthorized remote attacker to consume system resources and disrupt service availability.

Affected products:
- OpenSSH for Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70163






































































































































Related in this roundup: [CVE-2026-97816](#cve-2026-97816), [CVE-2026-83754](#cve-2026-83754), [CVE-2026-83769](#cve-2026-83769), [CVE-2026-83871](#cve-2026-83871).

## CVE-2026-70222

CVE-2026-70222 is a local privilege escalation vulnerability in the Windows Shell caused by a relative path traversal flaw. An authenticated attacker can exploit this vulnerability to gain higher privileges on the target system.

Affected products:
- Windows Shell

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70222





































































































































Related in this roundup: [CVE-2026-83782](#cve-2026-83782), [CVE-2026-83906](#cve-2026-83906).

## CVE-2026-83909

CVE-2026-83909 is an information disclosure vulnerability within the Windows Win32 Kernel Subsystem. An authorized attacker can exploit this flaw to gain unauthorized access to sensitive system information by executing code locally on the target system.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-83909

## CVE-2026-84009

A time-of-check time-of-use (TOCTOU) race condition vulnerability exists in the Windows Projected File System, which could allow a locally authenticated attacker to perform file tampering. Successful exploitation requires an attacker to win a race condition during file access operations.

Affected products:
- Windows Projected File System

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-84009






















































































Related in this roundup: [CVE-2026-85328](#cve-2026-85328).

## CVE-2026-84007

A vulnerability in the Remote Desktop Gateway Service allows an authenticated attacker to perform an out-of-bounds read, potentially leading to unauthorized information disclosure over the network.

Affected products:
- Remote Desktop Gateway Service

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-84007








































































































Related in this roundup: [CVE-2026-84012](#cve-2026-84012).

## CVE-2026-84012

CVE-2026-84012 is a remote code execution vulnerability in the Windows Remote Desktop Gateway Service caused by a double-free memory management error. An unauthenticated attacker can exploit this flaw by sending specifically crafted network requests to the target service, potentially leading to full system compromise.

Affected products:
- Remote Desktop Gateway Service

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-84012








































































































Related in this roundup: [CVE-2026-84007](#cve-2026-84007).

## CVE-2026-84011

CVE-2026-84011 describes a security feature bypass vulnerability in the Windows SMB Client caused by incorrect authorization. The vulnerability can be exploited locally by an authorized attacker to circumvent security controls.

Affected products:
- Windows SMB Client

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-84011

## CVE-2026-84013

CVE-2026-84013 is a local privilege escalation vulnerability in the Windows Telephony Server component caused by a use-after-free defect. An authorized attacker can exploit this flaw to execute code with elevated privileges on the target system.

Affected products:
- Windows Telephony Server

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-84013





































Related in this roundup: [CVE-2026-85810](#cve-2026-85810).

## CVE-2026-84014

CVE-2026-84014 is a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability in the Windows Device Association Service that enables an authorized local attacker to elevate their privileges to a higher integrity level.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-84014

A heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability exists in the Windows Universal Plug and Play (UPnP) Device Host service, which can be exploited by an authorized local attacker to achieve privilege escalation.

## CVE-2026-85803

CVE-2026-85803 describes a use-after-free vulnerability within the Windows Camera Frame Server Monitor, which enables a locally authorized attacker to escalate their privileges. Detection efforts should focus on monitoring for unauthorized process interactions or suspicious activities initiated by the Camera Frame Server service.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-85803

## CVE-2026-85807

The Windows USB Video Driver contains an integer overflow or wraparound vulnerability that can be exploited by an authorized local attacker to gain elevated privileges on the system.

Affected products:
- Windows USB Video Driver

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-85807

## CVE-2026-85808

CVE-2026-85808 is a denial-of-service vulnerability in the Windows Routing and Remote Access Service (RRAS) caused by an out-of-bounds read. An unauthorized remote attacker can trigger this vulnerability to crash the service, impacting network availability.

Affected products:
- Routing and Remote Access Service

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-85808






































Related in this roundup: [CVE-2026-85809](#cve-2026-85809), [CVE-2026-70057](#cve-2026-70057), [CVE-2026-84020](#cve-2026-84020), [CVE-2026-85322](#cve-2026-85322).

## CVE-2026-85810

A use-after-free vulnerability in the Windows Telephony Server component allows a locally authenticated attacker to escalate their privileges to a higher level of authority on the system.

Affected products:
- Windows Telephony Server

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-85810





































Related in this roundup: [CVE-2026-84013](#cve-2026-84013).

## CVE-2026-85811

CVE-2026-85811 is a use-after-free vulnerability in Microsoft's Network Policy Server (NPS) that enables an unauthenticated attacker on an adjacent network to achieve remote code execution. Detection engineers should monitor NPS service traffic and process behavior for anomalous activity originating from local network segments.

Affected products:
- Network Policy Server

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-85811




































Related in this roundup: [CVE-2026-100222](#cve-2026-100222), [CVE-2026-85806](#cve-2026-85806), [CVE-2026-83655](#cve-2026-83655), [CVE-2026-85371](#cve-2026-85371), [CVE-2026-85799](#cve-2026-85799).

## CVE-2026-85812

The Windows Bind Filter Driver contains an information disclosure vulnerability that allows an authorized attacker with local access to read sensitive files or directories that should otherwise be restricted. This vulnerability stems from improper access control within the filter driver.

Affected products:
- Windows Bind Filter Driver

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-85812



































Related in this roundup: [CVE-2026-83692](#cve-2026-83692).

- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-85815

## CVE-2026-85816

CVE-2026-85816 describes an out-of-bounds read vulnerability in the Windows Wi-Fi Driver that allows an authorized attacker on an adjacent network to read sensitive memory information. Detection engineers should monitor for abnormal activity or errors related to Wi-Fi driver communication and stack memory operations.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-85816

## CVE-2026-85821

The Windows Universal Disk Format File System Driver (UDFS) contains an out-of-bounds read vulnerability that allows an authenticated, local attacker to perform unauthorized information disclosure. This vulnerability originates from improper bounds checking within the file system driver, which could be leveraged to gain access to sensitive kernel-level memory information.

Affected products:
- Windows Universal Disk Format File System Driver

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-85821

## CVE-2026-85854

A use-after-free vulnerability in the Windows Desktop Window Manager (DWM) Core Library can be exploited by an authorized local attacker to achieve privilege escalation.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-85854

## CVE-2026-85855

CVE-2026-85855 is a use-after-free vulnerability within the Windows DWM (Desktop Window Manager) Core Library. The vulnerability enables a locally authenticated attacker to achieve privilege escalation by exploiting memory corruption during window management operations.

Affected products:
- Windows DWM Core Library

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-85855


Related in this roundup: [CVE-2026-83726](#cve-2026-83726).

## CVE-2026-85856

CVE-2026-85856 is a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability in the Windows Internet Connection Sharing (ICS) service that can be exploited by an authorized attacker on an adjacent network to achieve remote code execution.

Affected products:
- Internet Connection Sharing

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-85856
